```
1	// 物品注册表
2	export type ToolType = 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer';
3	// Buff 类型（药水饮用后生效）
4	export type PotionBuff = 'agility' | 'ironskin' | 'resistance' | 'thorns' | 'regen';
5	// 盔甲部位：0 头盔 1 胸甲 2 胫甲（与 Inventory.armorSlotReq 的格位对应）
6	export type ArmorSlot = 0 | 1 | 2;
7	
8	export interface ItemDef {
9	  key: string;
10	  name: string;
11	  maxStack: number;
12	  tile?: string;          // 放置的 tile key
13	  wall?: string;          // 放置的 wall key
14	  tool?: {
15	    type: ToolType;
16	    power?: number;       // 工具力（镐/斧/锤）
17	    damage?: number;      // 武器伤害
18	    speed?: number;       // 使用间隔 tick
19	    knockback?: number;
20	    reach?: number;       // 攻击/作用半径（px）
21	  };
22	  heal?: number;          // 食用/饮用回复
23	  potion?: {
24	    buff: PotionBuff;
25	    duration: number;     // 秒
26	    isHealType: boolean;  // 受"耐药性"封锁
27	  };
28	  accessory?: 'lucky_horseshoe' | 'feral_claws'; // 配饰效果
29	  armor?: { slot: ArmorSlot; defense: number };   // 盔甲（可穿装备/时装格，仅装备格计防御）
30	  value?: number;         // 钱币价值（铜币）
31	  desc?: string;
32	}
33	
34	export const ITEM_DEFS: ItemDef[] = [];
35	const byKey: Record<string, number> = {};
36	export const ITEM_BY_KEY: Record<string, number> = byKey;
37	
38	function item(d: Partial<ItemDef> & { key: string; name: string }) {
39	  byKey[d.key] = ITEM_DEFS.length;
40	  ITEM_DEFS.push({ maxStack: 999, ...d } as ItemDef);
41	}

94	// ---- 盔甲（铜/铁/银/金三件套，防御取原版）----
95	const armorTiers: Array<[string, string, [number, number, number]]> = [
96	  // key 前缀, 显示前缀, [头盔, 胸甲, 胫甲] 防御
97	  ['copper', '铜', [1, 2, 1]],
98	  ['iron', '铁', [2, 3, 2]],
99	  ['silver', '银', [3, 4, 3]],
100	  ['gold', '金', [4, 5, 4]],
101	];
102	const armorParts: Array<[string, string, ArmorSlot]> = [
103	  ['helmet', '头盔', 0], ['chainmail', '胸甲', 1], ['greaves', '胫甲', 2],
104	];
105	for (const [prefix, cn, defs] of armorTiers) {
106	  armorParts.forEach(([suffix, cnPart, slot], k) => {
107	    item({
108	      key: `${prefix}_${suffix}`, name: `${cn}${cnPart}`, maxStack: 1,
109	      armor: { slot, defense: defs[k] },
110	      desc: `防御 +${defs[k]}`,
111	    });
112	  });
113	}
114	
115	// ---- 工具（power：镐力/斧力；damage：武器伤害）----
116	const tiers: Array<[string, string, number, number, number]> = [
117	  // key 前缀, 显示前缀, 颜色档, 镐力, 斧力
118	  ['wood', '木', 0, 10, 15],
119	  ['copper', '铜', 1, 30, 40],
120	  ['iron', '铁', 2, 45, 55],
121	  ['silver', '银', 3, 60, 70],
122	  ['gold', '金', 4, 80, 90],
123	];
124	const swordDmg = [8, 12, 16, 21, 27];
125	for (const [prefix, cn, tier, pickP, axeP] of tiers) {
126	  item({
127	    key: `${prefix}_pickaxe`, name: `${cn}镐`, maxStack: 1,
128	    tool: { type: 'pick', power: pickP, speed: 46, reach: 2.6 * 16 },
129	  });
130	  item({
131	    key: `${prefix}_axe`, name: `${cn}斧`, maxStack: 1,
132	    tool: { type: 'axe', power: axeP, speed: 50, reach: 2.4 * 16 },
133	  });
134	  item({
135	    key: `${prefix}_sword`, name: `${cn}剑`, maxStack: 1,
136	    tool: { type: 'sword', damage: swordDmg[tier], speed: 42, knockback: 3.5, reach: 2.2 * 16 },
137	  });
138	  item({
139	    key: `${prefix}_hammer`, name: `${cn}锤`, maxStack: 1,
140	    tool: { type: 'hammer', power: pickP, speed: 48, reach: 2.4 * 16 },
141	    desc: '敲除背景墙',
142	  });
143	}

216	export interface ItemStack { id: number; stack: number; }
217	export function mkStack(key: string, stack = 1): ItemStack | null {
218	  const id = byKey[key];
219	  if (id === undefined) return null;
220	  return { id, stack };
221	}
```

```
1	// 原版劈砍/挖掘伤害积累系统：Terraria1405/HitTile.cs 移植。
2	// 每次挥击对目标格积累伤害（阈值 100 破坏），停止攻击后按 TTL 分档衰减：
3	//   TTL<48 → -2/帧, <36 → -5, <24 → -7, <12 → -10；归零清除（原版 Prune 语义）
4	// 槽位用插入序 Map 简化（原版 500 槽 + order 换序，行为等价：最近使用的存活最久）
5	export interface HitTileEntry { damage: number; ttl: number }
6	
7	export class HitTile {
8	  private data = new Map<string, HitTileEntry>();
9	  private static readonly CAP = 500;
10	  private static readonly TTL = 60;
11	
12	  /** 命中目标格：积累伤害并重置 TTL。返回积累后的伤害值 */
13	  addDamage(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type = 1): number {
14	    const key = `${x},${y},${type}`;
15	    let e = this.data.get(key);
16	    if (!e) {
17	      // 容量控制：超过上限淘汰最旧条目（原版环形缓冲的等价行为）
18	      if (this.data.size >= HitTile.CAP) {
19	        const oldest = this.data.keys().next().value;
20	        if (oldest !== undefined) this.data.delete(oldest);
21	      }
22	      e = { damage: 0, ttl: HitTile.TTL };
23	      this.data.set(key, e);
24	    }
25	    e.damage += amount;
26	    e.ttl = HitTile.TTL;
27	    // 最近使用移到末尾（LRU 语义）
28	    this.data.delete(key);
29	    this.data.set(key, e);
30	    return e.damage;
31	  }
32	
33	  /** 读取当前积累伤害（不改 TTL） */
34	  getDamage(x: number, y: number, type = 1): number {
35	    return this.data.get(`${x},${y},${type}`)?.damage ?? 0;
36	  }
37	
38	  /** 清除指定格（破坏完成/格已空时调用） */
39	  clear(x: number, y: number, type = 1) {
40	    this.data.delete(`${x},${y},${type}`);
41	  }
42	
43	  clearAll() {
44	    this.data.clear();
45	  }
46	
47	  /** 每帧衰减（原版 HitTile.Prune 分档） */
48	  update() {
49	    for (const [key, e] of [...this.data]) {
50	      e.ttl--;
51	      if (e.ttl <= 1) { this.data.delete(key); continue; }
52	      if (e.ttl < 12) e.damage -= 10;
53	      else if (e.ttl < 24) e.damage -= 7;
54	      else if (e.ttl < 36) e.damage -= 5;
55	      else if (e.ttl < 48) e.damage -= 2;
56	      if (e.damage < 0) this.data.delete(key);
57	    }
58	  }
59	}
60
```

```
710	        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
711	        this.cb.onToast(`${heldDef.name}：${p.duration} 秒`);
712	      }
713	    }
714	    this.mining = null;
715	  }
716	
717	  private toolCanBreak(type: number, power: number, toolType: string): boolean {
718	    const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
719	    if (!d) return false;
720	    if (toolType === 'pick') return d.pick >= 0 && power >= d.pick;
721	    if (toolType === 'axe') return d.axe >= 0;
722	    return false;
723	  }
724	
725	  private tryMine(tx: number, ty: number, power: number, toolType: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer', speed: number) {
726	    // 范围检查
727	    const dx = tx - Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE);
728	    const dy = ty - Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
729	    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 4.5) { this.mining = null; return; }
730	    const type = this.world.store.get(tx, ty);
731	    // 锤：敲除背景墙（无墙则无事发生；也可清装饰）
732	    if (toolType === 'hammer') {
733	      const wall = this.world.store.get(tx, ty) === 0 ? this.world.store.wall[this.world.store.idx(tx, ty)] : 0;
734	      if (type !== 0 && TILE_DEFS[type]?.decor) {
735	        this.breakTile(tx, ty);
736	        this.sfx.play('chop');
737	        this.mining = null;
738	        return;
739	      }
740	      if (!wall) { this.mining = null; return; }
741	      // HitTile 制（type 2 = 墙）：每挥一击，阈值 100
742	      if (this.tickCount - this.lastMineHitTick < Math.max(6, Math.floor(speed / 3))) {
743	        this.hardnessCache = 100;
744	        this.mining = { x: tx, y: ty, progress: this.hitTiles.getDamage(tx, ty, 2) };
745	        return;
746	      }
747	      this.lastMineHitTick = this.tickCount;
748	      const total = this.hitTiles.addDamage(tx, ty, Math.round(power * 2), 2); // 锤墙：木 5 击 / 铜 2 击
749	      this.hardnessCache = 100;
750	      this.mining = { x: tx, y: ty, progress: total };
751	      this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, '#5C4436', 2, 1.2);
752	      if (total >= 100) {
753	        this.hitTiles.clear(tx, ty, 2);
754	        this.world.store.setWall(tx, ty, 0);
755	        this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, '#5C4436', 8, 1.8);
756	        this.sfx.play('chop');
757	        this.mining = null;
758	      }
759	      return;
760	    }
761	    if (type === 0 || !this.toolCanBreak(type, power, toolType)) {
762	      // 装饰物（杂草等）任意工具/武器一下清掉并掉落
763	      if (type !== 0 && TILE_DEFS[type]?.decor && (toolType === 'pick' || toolType === 'axe')) {
764	        this.breakTile(tx, ty);
765	        this.sfx.play('chop');
766	        this.mining = null;
767	        return;
768	      }
769	      this.mining = null;
770	      return;
771	    }
772	    // ---- 原版 HitTile 制（Player.PickTile 移植）：每挥一击积累伤害，阈值 100 破坏 ----
773	    // 每挥一击（按工具速度节流），伤害 = 工具力 × 材质系数（GetPickaxeDamage 简化）
774	    if (this.tickCount - this.lastMineHitTick < Math.max(6, Math.floor(speed / 3))) {
775	      // 节流窗内：只刷新裂缝显示，不积累
776	      this.hardnessCache = 100;
777	      this.mining = { x: tx, y: ty, progress: this.hitTiles.getDamage(tx, ty) };
778	      return;
779	    }
780	    this.lastMineHitTick = this.tickCount;
781	    const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
782	    // 材质系数（对齐原版 GetPickaxeDamage 的分档方向）：土系软 ×1.4 / 石系 ×1 /
783	    // 矿石 ×0.55 / 树 ×1（斧伤害另有 1.5 倍工具系数）/ 家具软 ×2
784	    let mult = 1;
785	    if (type === T.TREE || IMPORTED_TREE_TYPES.has(type)) mult = 1;
786	    else if (d.decor || (d.framed && !d.solid)) mult = 2;
787	    else if (/ore_/i.test(d.key)) mult = 0.55;
788	    else if (/stone|brick|obsidian|ash|gem_|fossil|silt|slate/i.test(d.key)) mult = 1;
789	    else mult = 1.4; // 泥土/草/沙/雪等软质
790	    const dmg = Math.round((toolType === 'axe' ? power * 1.5 : power) * mult);
791	    const total = this.hitTiles.addDamage(tx, ty, dmg);
792	    this.hardnessCache = 100;
793	    this.mining = { x: tx, y: ty, progress: total }; // 裂缝显示 = 积累进度
794	    this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE + 8, d.mapColor, 2, 1.2);
795	    if (total >= 100) {
796	      this.hitTiles.clear(tx, ty);
797	      this.breakTile(tx, ty);
798	      this.mining = null;
799	      // 破坏完成音：按材质区分
800	      if (toolType === 'axe') this.sfx.play('chop');
801	      else if (/stone|ore|brick|obsidian|ash|gem_|fossil|silt/i.test(d.key)) this.sfx.play('tink');
802	      else this.sfx.play('dig');
803	    }
804	  }
805	
806	  private tileHardness(type: number): number {
807	    const d = TILE_DEFS[type];
808	    // 铜镐基准（52/tick）：泥土 ~0.25s / 石 ~0.6s / 铜矿 ~0.8s / 金矿 ~1.6s
809	    // 树（斧）：木斧 ~2s / 铜斧 ~1s / 金斧 ~0.4s
810	    if (type === T.TREE) return 3400;
811	    if (type === T.LEAVES) return 40;
812	    if (d.decor) return 60;
813	    if (d.framed && !d.solid) return 300;               // 工作台/铁砧等
814	    switch (type) {
815	      case T.DIRT: case T.GRASS: case T.SAND: case T.SNOW: return 780;
816	      case T.STONE: return 1870;
817	      case T.WOOD: case T.PLATFORM: return 1250;
818	      case T.ORE_COPPER: return 2500;
819	      case T.ORE_IRON: return 3100;
820	      case T.ORE_SILVER: return 4100;
821	      case T.ORE_GOLD: return 5000;
822	      default: return 1000;
823	    }
824	  }
825

827	  private breakTile(x: number, y: number) {
828	    const st = this.world.store;
829	    const type = st.get(x, y);
```

```
92	  private groundPhysics(game: GameHooks, jumpV = 0, accel = 0.12, maxSpd = 1, buoyant = false) {

100	          this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY * 0.3, MAX_FALL_SPEED);

112	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + GRAVITY, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
113	    this.vx = Math.max(-maxSpd, Math.min(maxSpd, this.vx + accel * Math.sign(this.vx || 1) * 0));
114	    if (jumpV && this.onGround) this.vy = -jumpV;
115	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
116	    this.vx *= this.onGround ? 0.8 : 0.98;
117	  }

119	  private slimeAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
120	    // 水中漂浮状态：漂在水面时也可蓄力起跳
121	    const st = game.world.store;
122	    const inWater = st.liquid[st.idx(Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))] > 80;
123	    const surfaced = inWater && Math.abs(this.vy) < 0.8; // 漂在水面附近（准地面）
124	    // 状态机：0 蓄力 → 1 跳跃
125	    if (this.state === 0) {
126	      this.vx *= 0.6;
127	      if (this.onGround) this.squash = Math.min(1, this.squash + 0.08);
128	      const canAct = this.onGround || surfaced;
129	      // 脱困冷却内（stuckCd>0）落地/浮面立刻再跳，不在原地等 50 tick 空转
130	      if (canAct && (this.aiT % 50 === 0 || this.stuckCd > 0 || (player && this.aiT > 40 && Math.abs(player.cx - this.cx) < TILE * 12))) {
131	        // 起跳方向：掉头冷却内用当前朝向（不再朝玩家死磕同一堵墙）
132	        let dir = this.facing;
133	        if (player && this.stuckCd === 0) {
134	          const dx = player.cx - this.cx;
135	          if (Math.abs(dx) > 8) dir = Math.sign(dx);
136	        }
137	        this.facing = dir;
138	        const dist = player ? Math.min(1, Math.abs(player.cx - this.cx) / (TILE * 10)) : 0.4;
139	        // 前方障碍高度探测（1-2 格）→ 按需起跳力度。
140	        // 普通跳 vy-4.2 在 GRAVITY=0.36 下脚底只升 24.5px（1.5 格）——
141	        // 精灵贴图放大 1.25 倍看着跳很高，但碰撞盒过不了 32px 的两格墙
142	        const fx = Math.floor((this.cx + dir * (this.w / 2 + 2)) / TILE);
143	        const fy = Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 1) / TILE);
144	        let obsH = 0;
145	        for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
146	          if (st.isSolid(fx, fy - k)) obsH = k + 1; else break;
147	        }
148	        const headroom = obsH > 0 && obsH <= 2 && !st.isSolid(fx, fy - obsH) && !st.isSolid(fx, fy - obsH - 1);
149	        if (headroom) {
150	          // 障碍跳：1 格 -4.6（脚底升 29px）/ 2 格 -6.0（升 50px），慢速横移保证越过块面
151	          this.vx = dir * (obsH === 1 ? 1.6 : 1.2);
152	          this.vy = obsH === 1 ? -4.6 : -6.0;
153	        } else {
154	          this.vx = dir * (1.6 + dist * 2.6);
155	          // 水中起跳加力：跃出水面爬上岸（普通力度会被浮力吃掉困在水里）
156	          this.vy = inWater ? -(5.6 + dist * 1.6) : -(4.2 + dist * 2.4);
157	        }
158	        this.jumpStartX = this.cx;
159	        this.state = 1;
160	        this.squash = -0.7;
161	      }
162	    } else {
163	      // 空中转向（与主角空操一致）：跳跃中持续朝面向水平加速——
164	      // 没有它起跳瞬间 vx 被墙清零后就只剩原地垂直跳，永远出不去
165	      this.vx = Math.max(-2.6, Math.min(2.6, this.vx + this.facing * 0.12));
166	      const landed = this.onGround && this.vy >= 0 && this.aiT > 10;
167	      if (landed || (surfaced && this.aiT > 20)) {
168	        // 起跳后几乎没位移（被墙弹回）→ 直接记一次失败，快速累计触发翻面
169	        if (Math.abs(this.cx - this.jumpStartX) < 6) this.stuckT += 10;
170	        this.state = 0;
171	        this.aiT = 0;
172	      }
173	    }
174	    this.groundPhysics(game, 0, 0.12, 1, true);
175	    // 卡墙脱困（水中/陆地通用）：撞墙计数用"缓慢衰减"而非硬清零——
176	    // 史莱姆是"跳一下撞墙→落回水中漂离"的循环，撞墙帧之间 hitWall 为 false，
177	    // 硬重置会让跨跳跃的撞墙永远攒不满阈值。累计 24 → 翻面 + 逃亡冷却
178	    // （水中 240 tick：足够连续多跳横渡水域脱离，否则冷却一到又朝墙对面的玩家折返死磕）
179	    if (this.stuckCd > 0) this.stuckCd--;
180	    if (this.hitWall) {

182	    } else {
183	      this.stuckT = Math.max(0, this.stuckT - 1);
184	    }
185	    if (this.stuckT > 24) {
186	      this.stuckT = 0;
187	      this.stuckCd = inWater ? 240 : 90;
188	      this.facing = -this.facing;
189	    }
190	  }
191	
192	  private zombieAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
193	    // 卡墙脱困：朝玩家走却持续撞墙（高墙跳不过去）→ 掉头往另一侧探索
194	    if (this.onGround && this.hitWall && player) {
195	      if (++this.stuckT > 80) {
196	        this.stuckT = 0;
197	        this.stuckCd = 90; // 掉头冷却：期间不再朝玩家折返
198	      }
199	    } else if (!this.hitWall) {
200	      this.stuckT = 0;
201	    }
202	    if (this.stuckCd > 0) this.stuckCd--;

204	    if (player && this.stuckCd === 0) {

211	    }

213	    this.vx += dir * 0.08;

215	    this.groundPhysics(game, 0);
216	  }
217	
218	  private flyAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null, steer: number) {
219	    // 恶魔眼式飞行 AI：
220	    // 游荡时自主调整航向（朝巡航点平滑转向、到点即换新目标、撞墙自动绕开）；
221	    // 玩家进入索敌范围后：朝向玩家明显加速逼近；脱离后回落游荡。
222	    const dist = player ? Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy) : Infinity;
223	    const aggro = 18 * 16; // 18 格索敌
224	    // 卡墙脱困：追击中持续撞墙/贴地/顶头 → 暂时退回游荡重新选航向（冷却内不再贴脸），
225	    // 防止顶在崖壁/坑洞里原地抖动
226	    if (this.stuckCd > 0) this.stuckCd--;
227	    if (this.state === 1 && (this.hitWall || this.onGround || this.hitHead)) {
228	      if (++this.stuckT > 18) { this.stuckT = 0; this.stuckCd = 45; this.state = 0; this.target = null; }
229	    } else this.stuckT = 0;
230	    if (player && dist < aggro && this.stuckCd === 0) {
231	      // ---- 追击：朝向玩家，加速逼近 ----
232	      const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
233	      const d = dist || 1;
234	      this.vx += (dx / d) * steer * 4.5;
235	      this.vy += (dy / d) * steer * 4.5;
236	      this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
237	      this.state = 1; // 标记追击态（脱离后回到游荡时重新选巡航点）
238	    } else {
239	      // ---- 游荡：自主巡航，到点换向 ----
240	      if (this.state === 1 || !this.target || Math.hypot(this.target.x - this.cx, this.target.y - this.cy) < 32) {
241	        this.state = 0;
242	        // 脱困冷却内：重选目标强制向上（120-260px）——困在谷底时随机目标仍在低位，永远飞不出去
243	        const escape = this.stuckCd > 0;
244	        this.target = {
245	          x: this.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * (escape ? 200 : 320),
246	          y: this.cy + (escape ? -(100 + Math.random() * 160) : (Math.random() - 0.5) * 220),
247	        };
248	      }
249	      const dx = this.target.x - this.cx, dy = this.target.y - this.cy;
250	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
251	      this.vx += (dx / d) * steer * 1.4;
252	      this.vy += (dy / d) * steer * 1.4;
253	      this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
254	    }
255	    const damp = 0.96;
256	    this.vx *= damp; this.vy *= damp;
257	    const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
258	    // 追击上限略高于游荡：游荡 1.8，追击 2.2（太快会贴脸躲不开）
259	    const maxSpd = this.key === 'servant_of_cthulhu' ? 4.2 : (this.state === 1 ? 2.2 : 1.8);
260	    if (spd > maxSpd) { this.vx = (this.vx / spd) * maxSpd; this.vy = (this.vy / spd) * maxSpd; }
261	    // 通用飞行避水（def.avoidWater 声明式开启）：避让时重选航向
262	    if (this.def.avoidWater && avoidWater(this, game.world)) {
263	      this.state = 0;
264	      this.target = null;
265	      return;
266	    }
267	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
268	    if (this.hitWall) { this.vx = -this.vx * 0.7; this.state = 0; this.target = null; } // 撞墙重新选向
269	    if (this.hitHead) this.vy = -this.vy * 0.7;
270	    if (this.onGround) this.vy = -Math.abs(this.vy) - 0.5;
271	  }
272	
273	  private eocAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
274	    if (!player) return;
275	    const T = TILE;
276	    const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
277	    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
278	
279	    // ---- 二阶段触发：半血以下且不处于冲刺/前摇 → 原地旋转三圈变身 ----
280	    if (this.phase === 1 && this.hp <= this.maxHp * 0.5 && (this.state === 0 || this.state === 3 || this.state === 4)) {
281	      this.state = 5; this.aiT = 0; this.spin = 0;
282	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
283	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#C02020', 24, 3);
284	    }
285	
286	    // ---- 玩家跑太远（含被地形隔开追不上）→ 传送到视口外上空，从远处飞回来 ----
287	    if (this.state !== 5 && !this.dashing && dist > 55 * T) {
288	      const cam = (game as unknown as { camera?: { viewW: number; viewH: number; zoom: number } }).camera;
289	      const halfX = cam ? cam.viewW / 2 / cam.zoom / T : 40;  // 视口半宽（格）
290	      const halfY = cam ? cam.viewH / 2 / cam.zoom / T : 25;  // 视口半高（格）
291	      const side = this.cx < player.cx ? -1 : 1;
292	      const maxX = game.world.w * T - this.w, maxY = game.world.h * T - this.h;
293	      // 水平超出视口边缘 6 格 + 垂直高于视口上缘 4 格（保证完全在画面外）
294	      this.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxX, player.cx + side * (halfX + 6) * T - this.w / 2));
295	      this.y = Math.max(2 * T, Math.min(maxY, player.cy - Math.max(halfY + 4, 14) * T));
296	      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;
297	      this.state = 0; this.aiT = 0;
298	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#802020', 20, 3);
299	    }
300	
301	    this.dashing = false;
302	    switch (this.state) {
303	      case 0: {
304	        // 悬浮/进场：远处直线飞近，近处绕玩家侧上方悬浮（带起伏）
305	        if (dist > 20 * T) {
306	          this.vx += (dx / dist) * 0.3;
307	          this.vy += (dy / dist) * 0.3;
308	        } else {
309	          const tx = player.cx - this.facing * 6 * T;
310	          const ty = player.cy - 4 * T;
311	          this.vx += Math.sign(tx - this.cx) * 0.12 + Math.sin(this.aiT * 0.05) * 0.04;
312	          this.vy += Math.sign(ty - this.cy) * 0.12 + Math.cos(this.aiT * 0.07) * 0.06;
313	        }
314	        this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
315	        this.vx *= 0.95; this.vy *= 0.95;
316	        // 仆从召唤
317	        const servantEvery = this.phase === 2 ? 260 : 420;
318	        if (this.aiT > 0 && this.aiT % servantEvery === 0) {
319	          game.spawnEnemy('servant_of_cthulhu', this.cx, this.cy);
320	        }
321	        // 就位 → 进入冲撞连段（3 连冲；二阶段蓄力更短）
322	        if (dist <= 24 * T && this.aiT > (this.phase === 2 ? 45 : 90)) {
323	          this.state = 1; this.aiT = 0; this.chargesLeft = 3;
324	        }
325	        break;
326	      }
327	      case 1: {
328	        // 前摇：短暂悬停后锁定玩家位置迅猛冲出
329	        this.vx *= 0.8; this.vy *= 0.8;
330	        if (this.aiT > (this.phase === 2 ? 8 : 14)) {
331	          this.state = 2; this.aiT = 0;
332	          const d2 = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy) || 1;
333	          const p2 = this.phase === 2 ? 1.6 : 1; // 二阶段更快更强
334	          this.vx = ((player.cx - this.cx) / d2) * 7 * p2;
335	          this.vy = ((player.cy - this.cy) / d2) * 7 * p2;
336	          this.facing = this.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;
337	          this.dashing = true;
338	        }
339	        break;
340	      }
341	      case 2: {
342	        // 冲撞：无视地形直接穿透，冲过一段距离后回头
343	        this.dashing = true;
344	        // 速度感拖尾粒子
345	        if (this.aiT % 2 === 0) {
346	          game.spawnParticles(
347	            this.cx - Math.sign(this.vx || 1) * 20, this.cy - Math.sign(this.vy || 1) * 14,
348	            this.phase === 2 ? '#FF4040' : '#D0D0D0', 2, 2,
349	          );
350	        }
351	        const spd = 7 * (this.phase === 2 ? 1.6 : 1);
352	        if (this.aiT * spd > 20 * T || this.aiT > 70) {
353	          this.chargesLeft--;
354	          this.state = 3; this.aiT = 0;
355	        }
356	        break;
357	      }
358	      case 3: {
359	        // 回头悬停：减速 → 还有剩余次数再冲，否则收招拉开距离
360	        this.vx *= 0.82; this.vy *= 0.82;
361	        if (this.aiT > (this.phase === 2 ? 12 : 20)) {
362	          if (this.chargesLeft > 0) { this.state = 1; this.aiT = 0; }
363	          else {
364	            // 收招落点：进入状态时一次性锁定（玩家反方向 10 格、抬高 3 格）。
365	            // 若每帧从当前位置重算，目标会随 Boss 一起外退 → 一直飞到计时上限
366	            const d0 = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy) || 1;
367	            this.target = {
368	              x: player.cx + ((this.cx - player.cx) / d0) * 10 * T,
369	              y: player.cy + ((this.cy - player.cy) / d0) * 10 * T - 3 * T,
370	            };
371	            this.state = 4; this.aiT = 0;
372	          }
373	        }
374	        break;
375	      }
376	      case 4: {
377	        // 收招：飞到锁定落点（贴近玩家 10 格处）后立刻回到悬浮，保持压迫感
378	        const away = this.target;
379	        if (away) {
380	          const adx = away.x - this.cx, ady = away.y - this.cy;
381	          const ad = Math.hypot(adx, ady) || 1;
382	          this.vx += (adx / ad) * 0.16;
383	          this.vy += (ady / ad) * 0.16;
384	          this.vx *= 0.96; this.vy *= 0.96;
385	          if (ad < 2.5 * T || this.aiT > 90) {
386	            this.state = 0; this.aiT = 0; this.target = null;
387	          }
388	        } else {
389	          this.state = 0; this.aiT = 0;
390	        }
391	        break;
392	      }
393	      case 5: {
394	        // 变身：原地旋转六圈（比三圈更快——100 tick 转 12π，角速度翻倍多）
395	        this.spin = Math.min(1, this.aiT / 100);
396	        this.vx *= 0.85; this.vy *= 0.85;
397	        this.visAngle += (Math.PI * 12) / 100; // 六圈自转
398	        if (this.aiT % 4 === 0) game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#C02020', 3, 2);
399	        if (this.aiT >= 100) {
400	          this.phase = 2;
401	          this.state = 0; this.aiT = 0; this.spin = 0;
402	          game.playSfx('roar');
403	          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF3030', 30, 3);
404	        }
405	        break;
406	      }
407	    }
408	
409	    // 显示角度平滑追踪移动方向（最短弧插值；悬浮低速时保持原朝向不抖动）
410	    if (this.state !== 5) {
411	      const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
412	      if (spd > 0.6) {
413	        const target = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + Math.PI * 1.5; // 素材默认朝左偏下 90°
414	        let diff = target - this.visAngle;
415	        while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= Math.PI * 2;
416	        while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += Math.PI * 2;
417	        this.visAngle += diff * 0.18;
418	      }
419	    }
420	
421	    // 移动：EoC 全程无视地形——悬浮飞行体贴图块边缘会卡顿、冲撞后残留在
422	    // 图块内也会受碰撞影响，统一直接积分 + 世界边界钳制（撞墙/嵌入问题全部消除）
423	    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;
424	    this.onGround = false; this.hitWall = false; this.hitHead = false;
425	    const maxX = game.world.w * T - this.w, maxY = game.world.h * T - this.h;
426	    this.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxX, this.x));
427	    this.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxY, this.y));
428	  }
429	
430	  /** 受击。返回是否死亡 */
431	  hurt(dmg: number, kbx: number, kby: number, game: GameHooks): boolean {
432	    if (this.iframes > 0) return false;
433	    this.hp -= dmg;
434	    this.iframes = 8;
435	    this.hpBarT = 240; // 受击后血条持续显示 4 秒
436	    const resist = this.def.knockbackResist;
437	    if (resist < 0.9) {
438	      this.vx += kbx * (1 - resist);
439	      this.vy += kby * (1 - resist);
440	    }
441	    if (this.hp <= 0) {
442	      this.dead = true;
443	      // 掉落
444	      const rng = new RNG(Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e9));
445	      for (const d of this.def.drops) {
446	        if (rng.next() < d.chance) {
447	          const n = rng.int(d.min, d.max);
448	          if (n > 0) game.spawnDrop(this.cx, this.cy, d.item, n, rng.range(-1.5, 1.5), -2);
449	        }
450	      }
451	      game.onEnemyKilled(this.key);
452	      // 死亡专属声：Boss 咆哮；普通怪优先专属 killedSound，无则通用
453	      if (this.def.boss) game.playSfx('roar');
454	      else if (this.def.killedSound.length) game.playSfxFiles(this.def.killedSound);
455	      else game.playSfx('killed');
456	      // 死亡碎裂：与主角同款物理粒子（喷溅/落地反弹/停驻淡出），颜色按怪物类型
457	      const [c1, c2, c3] = this.def.gore;
458	      const big = this.def.boss || this.w > 40;
459	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.y + this.h * 0.3, c1, big ? 26 : 12, big ? 3.4 : 2.6, { life: 240, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
460	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, c2, big ? 18 : 8, big ? 2.6 : 2.0, { life: 220, damp: 0.985, grav: 0.09 });
461	      game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy - this.h * 0.2, c3, big ? 12 : 6, big ? 3.0 : 2.3, { life: 200, damp: 0.988, grav: 0.09 });
462	      return true;
463	    }
464	    // 受击：少量碎屑零散溅落（不大面积）
465	    game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, this.def.gore[0], 5, 1.4, { life: 60 });
466	    return false;
467	  }
468	
469	  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
470	}
```

```
1206	  private trySpawnEnemy() {
1207	    const w = this.world;
1208	    const p = this.player;
1209	    if (p.dead) return;
1210	    const isDay = w.clock.isDay;
1211	    const count = this.entities.enemies.filter((e) => !(e as Enemy).def.boss).length;
1212	    if (count >= ENEMY_CAP) return;
1213	    if (this.boss) return;
1214	
1215	    const rng = new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0);
1216	    // 生成点：玩家周围环带
1217	    const ang = rng.range(0, Math.PI * 2);
1218	    const dist = rng.range(SPAWN_MIN_DIST, SPAWN_MAX_DIST);
1219	    const ex = p.cx + Math.cos(ang) * dist;
1220	    const ey = p.cy + Math.sin(ang) * dist;
1221	    const tx = Math.floor(ex / TILE), ty = Math.floor(ey / TILE);
1222	    const st = w.store;
1223	    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty) || tx < 2 || tx > st.w - 3) return;
1224	
1225	    const isUnderground = st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)] !== 0 || ty > w.rockLevel;
1226	    // 海洋/水域排除：该列从天空到 rockLevel 全是液体 = 海里——除鱼类外不生成
1227	    // （蝙蝠等飞行怪在海面上盘旋也不合理，一并排除）
1228	    {
1229	      let liquidTop = -1;
1230	      for (let y = 2; y < Math.min(w.rockLevel, st.h); y++) {
1231	        if (st.liquid[st.idx(tx, y)] > 40) { liquidTop = y; break; }
1232	      }
1233	      if (liquidTop >= 0) {
1234	        // 水面之下 10 格仍是液体 → 判定为海/深水区
1235	        let deep = true;
1236	        for (let y = liquidTop; y < Math.min(liquidTop + 10, st.h); y++) {
1237	          if (st.liquid[st.idx(tx, y)] <= 40) { deep = false; break; }
1238	        }
1239	        if (deep) return;
1240	      }
1241	    }
1242	    // 地狱区：熔岩史莱姆 / 熔岩僵尸（不怕岩浆）
1243	    const hellTop = Math.floor(st.h * 0.86);
1244	    let key: string | null = null;
1245	    if (ty > hellTop) {
1246	      key = rng.chance(0.55) ? 'lava_slime' : 'magma_zombie';
1247	    } else if (isUnderground) {
1248	      key = rng.chance(0.6) ? 'cave_bat' : (rng.chance(0.5) ? 'slime_blue' : 'slime_green');
1249	    } else if (isDay) {
1250	      key = 'slime_green';
1251	    } else {
1252	      // 夜间地表：僵尸/恶魔眼/史莱姆——洞穴蝙蝠不在此列（只在洞里）
1253	      const r = rng.next();
1254	      key = r < 0.4 ? 'zombie' : r < 0.75 ? 'demon_eye' : 'slime_blue';
1255	    }
1256	    const def = ENEMY_DEFS[key];
1257	    if (def.nightOnly && isDay) return;
1258	    if (def.dayOnly && !isDay && !isUnderground) { /* 史莱姆夜间也可少量 */ }
1259	    // 找落脚点：从 ty 向下找空中有地面的位置
1260	    let spawnY = -1;

1262	      const yy = ty + dy;

1267	      const clear = !st.isSolid(tx, yy) && !st.isSolid(tx, yy - 1) && dry;

1322	  private isValidHouse(doorX: number, doorY: number): boolean {
1323	    // 门占 3 格（doorY 为门顶），检查门内空间：有光源 + 有实心地板 + 有背景墙
1324	    // doorY+3 为地板行
1325	    const st = this.world.store;
1326	    let light = false, solidFloor = 0, walls = 0;
1327	    for (let dx = 1; dx <= 12; dx++) {
1328	      for (let dy = -6; dy <= 4; dy++) {
1329	        const x = doorX + dx, y = doorY + dy;
1330	        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
1331	        if (st.get(x, y) === T.TORCH) light = true;
1332	        if (dy === 3 && st.isSolid(x, y)) solidFloor++;
1333	        if (dy >= -2 && dy <= 2 && st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0) walls++;
1334	      }
1335	    }
1336	    return light && solidFloor >= 6 && walls >= 20;
1337	  }
1338	
1339	  // ================= GameHooks 实现 =================
1340	
1341	  spawnDrop(x: number, y: number, itemKey: string, count = 1, vx = 0, vy = -2) {
1342	    const id = ITEM_BY_KEY[itemKey];
1343	    if (id === undefined) return;
1344	    const drop = new ItemDrop(x - 6, y - 6, id, count, vx, vy);
1345	    this.entities.add(drop, 'drops');
1346	  }
1347	
1348	  damagePlayer(dmg: number, srcX: number, srcY: number, attacker?: Enemy) {
1349	    const p = this.player;
1350	    if (p.dead) return;
1351	    const ok = p.damage(dmg, srcX, srcY);
1352	    if (ok) {
1353	      // 致死一击：不播受击声，只播死亡声（保证最后听到的音效是死亡）
1354	      if (p.hp <= 0) this.sfx.play('pkilled');
1355	      else this.sfx.play('hurt');
1356	      // 实际扣血 = max(1, dmg - defense*0.5)（在 Player.damage 内）
1357	      const dealt = Math.max(1, dmg - p.defense * 0.5);
1358	      this.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, Math.round(dealt), false, '#FF5050');
1359	      // 荆棘 Buff：受击反弹 2 伤害（移植自 Maples Player.Thorn）
```

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Good — I have the vanilla formulas:
- Hammer on tileHammer tiles: damage = num1 + sItem.hammer (num1=100 if tileNoFail)
- Axe on tileAxe (trees): damage = num1 + axe*1.2 (or axe*3*1.2 for cactus, tile 80)
- Pick on other: GetPickaxeDamage → num1 + pickPower (most tiles), +pickPower*2 for tiles 48/232, /2 altar etc., plus another +pickPower for dirt/grass/sand etc. types (147,0,40,53,57,59,123,224,397).

```

````
## A3. Combat usage (`Game.ts`, `Enemy.ts`, `Player.ts`)

- **Player → enemy** (`Game.ts:1180-1200`, swing resolution): `baseDmg = tool?.damage ?? max(1, ceil((tool?.power ?? 10)/16))` (pick/axe fallback, L1194); `crit = rand < 0.08` (L1192); `dmg = max(1, round((baseDmg + player.meleeDamageBonus) * (crit?2:1) * (0.9+rand*0.2)))` (L1195); knockback passed as `kdir*3.2, -2.5` (L1197). **Enemy defense is never applied** — `Enemy.hurt` (`Enemy.ts:431-466`) does `hp -= dmg`, i-frames 8 ticks, kb scaled by `(1 - knockbackResist)`, skipped entirely if `resist >= 0.9` (L437).
- **Enemy → player**: contact AABB in `Enemy.fixedUpdate` (`Enemy.ts:77-79`) → `game.damagePlayer(def.damage, ...)` → `Game.damagePlayer` (`Game.ts:1348-1358`) → `Player.damage` (`Player.ts:359-372`): `reduction = max(1, dmg - defense*0.5)`; `defense = buffs.defenseBonus + equipped armor defense` (`Player.ts:71-77`). Knockback ±3.4 / -3 on player.
- **Spawn logic** (`Game.trySpawnEnemy`, `Game.ts:1206-1279`): cap `ENEMY_CAP=9` non-boss (`constants.ts:30`), check every `SPAWN_CHECK_INTERVAL=30` ticks (L37), ring position `42*TILE..72*TILE` (L35-36); `isUnderground = wall != 0 || ty > rockLevel` (L1225); hell band `ty > floor(h*0.86)` → lava_slime 55% / magma_zombie (L1243-1246); underground → cave_bat 60% else blue/green slime (L1247-1248); day surface → slime_green (L1250); night surface → zombie 0.4 / demon_eye 0.35 / slime_blue 0.25 (L1253-1254). Excludes ocean columns (L1228-1241), requires dry footing (flying: any non-solid dry air, L1269-1270; ground: 2 clear + solid below, L1267-1273), searches `ty-8..ty+12`. `nightOnly` blocked during day (L1257) and despawned at dayFactor>0.85 (`Enemy.ts:82-84`); despawn beyond 90 tiles (`Enemy.ts:86-89`).

## A4. HitTile integration in `Game.tryMine` (`Game.ts:725-804`)

`HitTile` itself (`HitTile.ts`): threshold 100 damage, 500-slot LRU cap, TTL 60, decay `-2/-5/-7/-10` per frame by TTL band (`HitTile.ts:48-58`) — matches vanilla Prune.

- **Throttle** (`Game.ts:774`): `tickCount - lastMineHitTick < max(6, floor(speed/3))` → only refreshes crack display. Note `tryMine` is called with a **fixed `speed=22`** (`Game.ts:663`), so effective throttle is `max(6, 7) = 7` ticks regardless of tool; hammer path same formula (L742).
- **Material multipliers** (`Game.ts:784-790`): tree/imported-tree `1`; decor or `framed && !solid` (furniture) `2`; `/ore_/i` `0.55`; `/stone|brick|obsidian|ash|gem_|fossil|silt|slate/i` `1`; else soft `1.4`.
- **Per-hit damage** (`Game.ts:790`): `dmg = round((toolType === 'axe' ? power*1.5 : power) * mult)`; break at `addDamage(...) >= 100` (L795).
- **Hammer/wall path** (L732-759): `addDamage(tx, ty, round(power*2), type 2)`, threshold 100 ("木 5 击 / 铜 2 击" per comment).
- Range gate: `hypot(dx,dy) > 4.5` tiles from player (L729).

## A5. Our enemy ↔ vanilla NPC mapping

`slime_green`→Green Slime (net -3), `slime_blue`→Blue Slime (type 1), `zombie`→Zombie (3), `demon_eye`→Demon Eye (2), `cave_bat`→Cave Bat (49), `lava_slime`→Lava Slime (59), `eye_of_cthulhu`→Eye of Cthulhu (4), `servant_of_cthulhu`→Servant of Cthulhu (5), `magma_zombie`→**no vanilla analog** (custom; closest Hellbat 60 / zombie recolor).

# B. Vanilla 1.4.0.5 (`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1405`)

## B8. Combat formulas

- **`Main.CalculateDamageNPCsTake`** (`Main.cs:49538-49544`): `dmg = Damage − Defense*0.5`, floor 1. Used by `NPC.StrikeNPC` (`NPC.cs:46824-46836`): `Defense = max(0, defense − (ichor?20:0) − (betsysCurse?40:0))` → formula above → `crit *= 2` → `takenDamageMultiplier`.
- **`Main.CalculateDamagePlayersTake`** (`Main.cs:49554-49564`): normal `Damage − Defense*0.5`; expert `Damage − Defense*0.75`; master `Damage − Defense`; floor 1. Used in `Player.Hurt` (`Player.cs:24791-24794`). (So your current `max(1, dmg − def*0.5)` is exactly vanilla normal mode.)
- **NPC → player contact** (`Player.cs:19855-19871`): `Damage = Main.DamageVar(npc.damage * damageMultiplier, -luck)` → `Hurt(...)`; banner buffs scale it down. **No halving of NPC damage exists in 1.4.0.5** — the "halved" memory is old pre-1.4 behavior; the raw SetDefaults damage above is what lands (on classic).
- **`Main.DamageVar`** (`Main.cs:49517-49536`): `dmg * (1 + rand(-15..15)*0.01)`, luck rerolls, then `Math.Round` — i.e. ±15% variance (your `0.9 + rand*0.2` ≈ ±10%).
- **Weapon damage** (`Player.GetWeaponDamage`, `Player.cs:34154-34177`): `int(damage * meleeDamage + 5e-7)` (class multipliers truncate); base `meleeCrit = 4` (`Player.cs:170`).
- **Mining damage** (`Player.GetPickaxeDamage`, `Player.cs:34472-34530`, consumed by `PickTile` at 34367-34381, threshold `hitTile.AddDamage(...) >= 100`): base 0 (or 100 for `tileNoFail` tiles) `+ pickPower` for normal tiles; `+ pickPower/2` demon altar/minecart track; `+ pickPower*2` for types 48/232; **dirt-family tiles (0, 40, 53, 57, 59, 123, 147, 224, 397) get `+ pickPower` AGAIN** (line 34525-34526) → effectively 2× pick power on dirt/grass/sand. Hard gates zero the damage below pickPower thresholds (e.g. Hellstone 65, ebonstone 65, dungeon brick 100). Trees/axe path (`Player.cs:29576-29579`): `base + axe*1.2` (cactus: `base + axe*3*1.2`); hammer tiles (`Player.cs:29546-29557`): `base + hammer`, break at 100.
````
